    GROUP PROFILE
    集團簡介



    Time Interconnect Technology Limited (the “Company”, together with             匯聚科技有限公司（「本公司」，連同其附屬公
    its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is a well-established supplier of customised    司統稱「本集團」）是一家信譽卓著的定制電線
    interconnect solutions with over 30 years’ experience in the industry. The     互連方案供應商 ，在行業擁有超過30年經驗 。
    Group is headquartered in Hong Kong, and has manufacturing facilities          本集團的總部設於香港 ，並於中華人民共和國
    in Shanghai, Suzhou, Jiangxi and Huizhou, the People’s Republic of China      （「中國」）上海 、蘇州 、江西及惠州以及墨西哥
    (“PRC”) and Mexico.                                                            擁有生產設施 。

    The Group currently manufactures and supplies a wide variety of copper        本集團目前製造及供應各種銅纜和光纜電線組
    & optical fiber cable assemblies, digital cable products and servers          件 、數字電線產品及服務器 ，產品均根據個別
    which are produced to the specifications and designs of its individual        客戶夥伴的詳述規格及設計而生產 。產品已使
    customer partners. Its products are used by a number of established           用在包括電訊 、數據中心 、工業設備 、醫療設
    PRC and international customers in a variety of market sectors, including     備 、汽車配線 、數字電線及服務器在內的各種
    telecommunication, data centre, industrial equipment, medical equipment,      市場領域 ，均受不少商譽優良的中國及國際客
    automotive wire harness, digital cable and server.                            戶所採用 。

    Its business features on customised cable and server products for a variety   本集團的業務特點為定制電線及服務器產品 ，
    of specifications which are used in a wide range of applications. The Group   適用於各種應用中的各種規格 。本集團按合約
    supplies cable and server products on a Contract Manufacturing Services       生產服務(CMS)基準供應電線及服務器產品 ，
    (CMS) basis, and its cable and server products are made-to-order, upon        而其電線及服務器產品在收到客戶訂單後定制
    receipt of its customers’ orders.                                             生產 。

    VISION                                                                        願景
    To be a leading provider of electronic interconnect products, continually      成為電纜互連產品的領先供應商 ，持續公認為
    recognised as a “Preferred Supplier” capable of delivering world class        「優選供應商」，積極地提供世界一流的品質 、
    quality, products and services – EACH and EVERY TIME.                          產品和服務 。

    STRATEGY                                                                      策略
    To become a Tier-1 supplier of cable products and high performance            成為5G電訊 、數據通訊 、醫療設備 、運輸及
    interconnect products to the market leaders in the 5G telecommunication,      工業領域的市場領導者之電線產品和高性能互
    data communication, healthcare, transport and industrial sectors.             連產品的一級供應商 。

continuous growth and core competitiveness in its market and to enable             供應商 ，為股東創造更大的價值 。對此 ，立訊

Gearing Ratio                                                                 負債比率
Gearing ratio is calculated as net debt (defined as bank loans, loans from    負債比率根據債務淨額（定義為銀行貸款 、來
parent company and lease liabilities less bank balances and cash and          自母公司的貸款以及租賃負債減銀行結餘及現
pledged deposits) divided by the sum of net debt and total equity and         金及已抵押存款）除以債務淨額及總權益之和
multiplied by 100%. As at 31 December 2023, the Group’s gearing ratio         再乘以100%計算 。於二零二三年十二月三十
was 55.3% as compared to the Previous Year 64.9%, the decrease was            一日 ，本集團的負債比率為55.3% ，而上年度
mainly attributable to the decrease of loans provided from Luxshare Group     則為64.9% ，減少主要歸因於立訊集團為本集
and bank loans for financing the Group’s operating working capital.           團的經營營運資金提供貸款及銀行貸款均有所
                                                                              減少 。

FOREIGN EXCHANGE EXPOSURE                                                     外匯風險
Most of the Group’s receipts and payments are denominated in United           本集團大部分收付款以美元 、港元 、人民幣及
States dollars, Hong Kong dollars, Renminbi and Euro. The Group’s             歐元計值 。本集團管理層透過訂立遠期外匯合
management monitors the risk of related foreign exchange exposure             約監察其相關外匯風險 。匯率波動可能對本集
by entering into forward foreign exchange contracts. Foreign currency         團業績產生影響 。本集團管理層持續評估本集
exchange rates are volatile and may have an impact on the Group’s             團的外幣風險 ，並於必要時採取措施盡量降低
results. The Group’s management evaluates the Group’s foreign currency        本集團的風險 。
exposure on a continuing basis and takes actions to minimise the Group’s
exposure whenever necessary.

TREASURY POLICIES                                                             庫務政策
As an internal treasury policy, the Group continues to implement a prudent    作為內部庫務政策 ，本集團繼續就財務管理政
policy on financial management policy and does not participate in any high-   策實施審慎政策 ，並無參與任何高風險投機活
risk speculative activities. However, the Group’s management monitors         動 。然而 ，本集團管理層會監察外匯風險 ，並
exchange exposure and will consider hedging significant foreign currency      將於需要時考慮對沖重大外幣風險 。本集團亦
exposure should the need arise. The Group will also monitor and maintain      將監察及維持港元現金結餘 ，以盡量減少對可
a Hong Kong dollar cash balance to minimise the need for unnecessary          能引致匯兌虧損的不必要外匯兌換的需求 。
foreign exchange conversion which may result in exchange loss.

The reporting currency of the Group is presented in Hong Kong dollars,        本集團的呈報貨幣為港元 ，乃由於董事認為 ，
as the Directors consider that it is more relevant to the users of the        此舉對綜合財務報表的使用者更為貼合 ，原因
consolidated financial statements as the Company listed its shares on the     為本公司股份於聯交所上市 。
Stock Exchange.

     The Company adopts a general dividend policy that aims to provide shareholders of         本公司採納一般股息政策 ，旨在於任何財政年度向
     the Company out of the Group’s profit attributable to shareholders in any financial       本公司股東提供本集團之股東應佔溢利 ，惟須遵守

     limitation restrictions on dividend declaration and payment) under the Companies          綱及細則的所有適用規定（包括但不限於股息宣派及
     Ordinance (Cap. 622 of the Laws of Hong Kong) and the memorandum and articles             派付限制）。
     of association of the Company.

     INTRODUCTION                                                                          緒言
     The Board and the management of the Company are committed to the maintenance          董事會及本公司管理層致力於維持良好的企業管治
     of good corporate governance practices and procedures. The Company believes           常規及程序 。本公司相信 ，良好的企業管治為有效
     that good corporate governance provides a framework that is essential for             管理 、健康的企業文化 、成功的業務增長及提升股
     effective management, a healthy corporate culture, successful business growth         東價值提供必要的框架 。本公司企業管治原則強調
     and enhancing shareholders’ value. The corporate governance principles of the         優質的董事會 、完善的內部控制 、具透明度及對全
     Company emphasise a quality Board, sound internal controls, transparency and          體股東負責 。
     accountability to all shareholders.

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICE                                                         企業管治常規
     The Company acknowledges the need and importance of corporate governance              本公司確信企業管治是為股東創造價值之必要及重

CULTURE AND VALUES                                                                       文化及價值觀
A healthy corporate culture across the Group is integral to attain its vision and        於本集團內營造健康的企業文化對實現其願景及策
strategy. It is the Board’s role to foster a corporate culture with the following core   略是不可或缺的 。董事會的責任營造揉合以下核心
principles and to ensure that the Company’s vision, values and business strategies       原則的企業文化 ，以確保本公司的願景 、價值觀及
are aligned to it.                                                                       業務策略與企業文化保持一致 。

1. Integrity and code of conduct                                                         1. 誠信及操守準則
   The Group strives to maintain high standards of business ethics and corporate            本集團致力於在所有活動及營運中維持高水平的
   governance across all our activities and operations. The Directors, management           商業道德及企業管治 。董事 、管理層及員工均
   and staff are all required to act lawfully, ethically and responsibly, and the           須以合法 、符合道德及負責任的方式行事 ，而
   required standards and norms are explicitly set out in the training materials for        所要求的標準及規範已明確載列於所有新員工的
   all new staff and embedded in various policies such as the Group’s employee              培訓資料中 ，並包括各項政策 ，例如本集團的
   handbook (including therein the Group’s code of conduct), the anti-corruption            員工手冊（包括本集團的行為守則）、本集團的
   policy and the whistleblowing policy of the Group. Trainings are conducted               反貪污政策及舉報政策等 。本公司不時進行培
   from time to time to reinforce the required standards in respect of ethics and           訓 ，以加強道德操守及誠信方面的規定標準 。
   integrity.

2. Commitment                                                                            2. 承擔
   The Group believes that the culture of commitment to workforce development,              本集團相信 ，致力於員工發展 、工作場所安全
   workplace safety and health, diversity, and sustainability is one where people           與健康 、多樣性及可持續性的文化使人們產生一
   have a feeling of commitment and emotional engagement with the Group’s                   種承擔感及對本集團使命的情感投入 。此為打造
   mission. This sets the tone for a strong, productive workforce that attracts,            強大高效的員工隊伍奠定了基調 ，可吸引 、培
   develops, and retains the best talent and produces the highest quality                   養並挽留最優秀的人才 ，交付最優質的工作 。
   work. Moreover, the Company’s strategy in the business development and                   此外 ，本公司於業務發展及管理的策略是實現長
   management are to achieve long-term, steady and sustainable growth, while                遠 、穩定及可持續的增長 ，同時從環境 、社會
   having due considerations from environment, social and governance aspects.               及管治方面作出適當的考慮 。

1    PURPOSE                                                                              1   目的
     1.1 the Nomination Policy aims to set out the approach to guide the Nomination           1.1 提名政策旨在載列提名委員會甄選 、委任及
         Committee in relation to the selection, appointment and re-appointment of                重新委任董事的指引方法 。
         the Directors.

2    CRITERIA                                                                             2   標準
     The Nomination Committee will evaluate, select and recommend candidate(s)                提名委員會將適當考慮下列標準（統稱為「標準」）

professional qualifications or related financial management experience. None of the       獨立非執行董事概無於本公司或其任何附屬公司擔

of its subsidiaries or is interested in any shares of the Company. The Company has        益 。本 公司 已接 獲各 獨立 非 執行董 事之 年度確認
received from each independent non-executive Director an annual confirmation of           書 ，確認其獨立性 ，本公司根據上市規則第3.13條
his independence, and the Company considers such Directors to be independent in           所載準則 ，認為該等董事均為獨立人士 。

     SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHT                                                                      股東權利

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                    風險管理及內部監控
The Directors acknowledge that they have overall responsibility for overseeing the      董事明白彼等有全盤責任監督本公司內部監控 、財
Company’s internal control, financial control and risk management system and shall      務監控及風險管理系統 ，並須持續監察其有效性 。
monitor its effectiveness on an ongoing basis. A review of the effectiveness of the     董事會至少每年對風險管理及內部監控系統的有效
risk management and internal control systems has been conducted by the Board at         性進行審閱 。
least annually.

Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material errors, losses or fraud,       本公司著力提供合理保證 ，避免出現重大錯誤 、損
the Company has established a risk management procedures which comprised the            失或造假 ，為此已成立風險管理程序 ，包括以下步
following steps:                                                                        驟：

–    Identify risks: Identify major and significant risks that could affect the         –   識別風險：識別可能影響達成本集團目標之主要
     achievement of goals of the Group;                                                     及重大風險；

–    Risk assessment: Assess and evaluate the identified risk according to its likely   –   風險評估：根據已識別風險的預料影響及出現之
     impact and the likelihood of occurrence;                                               可能性作出評估及評核；

–    Risk mitigation: Develop effective control activities to mitigate the risks.       –   紓緩風險：策劃有效的監管活動 ，務求紓緩風
                                                                                            險。

Risk identification and assessment is performed or updated annually, and the results    每年進行或更新風險識別及評估 ，風險評估 、評核
of risk assessment, evaluation and mitigation of each functions or operation are        的結果及各功能或營運之紓緩措施會詳細記錄在風
documented in the Risk Registry to communicate to the Board and Management              險資料冊內 ，以供董事會及管理層審閱 。
for reviews.

The Group’s risk management and internal control systems are, however, designed         然而 ，本集團之風險管理及內部監控系統之設計旨
to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives,     在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風險 ，僅可就
and can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material             重大失實聲明或損失提供合理而非絕對保證 。
misstatement or loss.

     Climate Change                                                                   氣候變化
     Climate change is one of the most critical and complicated challenges            氣候變化是新世紀全球所面臨最嚴峻和複雜的
     faced globally in the new century. Global warming leads to more frequent         挑戰之一 。全球暖化導致出現更頻繁的極端天
     extreme weather conditions including changes in precipitation mode,              氣狀況 ，包括降雨模式變化 、乾旱 、水災和叢
     droughts, floods and bushfires. Rise in sea level may inundate coastal           林大火 。水位上升將可能淹沒沿海地區 。面對
     areas. Faced with all sorts of natural disaster, individuals, corporations and   各種自然災害 ，個人 、企業和政府必須立即採
     governments must take immediate actions to tackle climate change.                取行動應對氣候變化 。

     In response to the climate change related risks and opportunities that brings    為應對氣候變化給本集團運營帶來的相關風險
     to the operation of the Group, the Board at least annually holds meeting         與機遇 ，董事會至少每年舉行一次會議 ，監督
     to oversee climate-related strategies, policies, actions and disclosure, and     與氣候相關的策略 、政策 、行動及披露 ，並制
     develops the related measures responding to the identified risks, in order       定相關措施應對已識別的風險 ，以降低負面影
     to mitigate the negative impact. The measures will be reviewed regularly         響 。該等措施將會被定期審查 ，以評估其是否
     to assess their effectiveness.                                                   有效執行 。

     Over-emission of greenhouse gases is the main factor in causing global           溫室氣體的過度排放是造成全球氣候變化的主
     climate change. To achieve a low carbon economy, the Group is committed          要因素 。為實現低碳經濟 ，本集團致力於透
     to reducing the greenhouse gas emissions through the approaches of               過緩和及適應方針減少溫室氣體排放 。舉例而
     mitigation and adaptation. For example, the Group has strived to mitigate        言 ，本集團透過採取各種環境政策和措施 ，致
     the risks brought by climate change through the adoption of various              力減輕氣候變化所帶來的風險；並推廣節能作
     environmental policies and measures; and promoting energy saving                 業 。本集團亦已考慮氣候變化對其日常營運帶
     practices. The Group has also considered potential physical risks of climate     來實際的潛在風險 ，如風暴 、火災或熱浪 ，並
     change to the daily operations such as storms, fires or heatwaves and            實施相關保護措施將風險降至最低 。
     implemented the relevant protection measures to minimise the risks.

Health and Safety                                                                   健康與安全
The Group strongly believes that ensuring a stable and safe production              本集團堅信 ，確保維持一個穩定與安全的生產
environment is the most important social responsibility to the stakeholders         環境是本集團對持份者最重要的社會責任 ，亦
and also one of the major priorities in the corporate management. The               是企業管理的優先重任之一 。下列為本集團內
health and safety measures listed below are some examples already taken             部已實施的部分健康及安全措施例子：
in place within the Group:

•    Sophisticated occupational health and safety management systems                •   在中國的生產設施建立精密的職業健康及
     have been established in the PRC production facilities;                            安全管理體系；

•    Canopies and other protection devices have been installed to ensure            •   已安裝保護罩及其他防護設施以確保生產
     the safe operation of production machinery and equipment;                          機器及設備安全運作；

•    Trainings on fire safety and fire drills are organised at the PRC              •   於中國的生產設施組織有關消防安全及火
     production facilities;                                                             警演習的培訓；

•    Health checks are conducted on a regular basis at the PRC production           •   中國的生產設施定期為僱員進行健康檢
     facilities to monitor employees’ health;                                           查 ，以監測僱員健康狀況；

•    Comprehensive medical insurance including outpatient treatments and            •   為總部僱員提供包括門診及其他健康的全
     other wellness is provided to the headquarters’ employees;                         面醫療保險；

•    Fire-fighting facilities, e.g. fire extinguishers, smoke detectors and etc.,   •   中國的生產設施及總部已安裝防火設備 ，
     are equipped at the PRC production facilities and headquarters;                    例如滅火筒 、煙霧感應器等；

•    Exhaust air treatment system has been installed in Linkz Suzhou to             •   華迅蘇州已安裝廢氣處理系統以消除生產
     eliminate volatile organic compounds emissions generated during                    過程中所產生的揮發性有機化合物排放；
     production processes; and                                                          及

•    The production facilities in the Huizhou installed a total of eight            •   惠州的生產設施已在屋頂上安裝合共八個
     activated carbon filter boxes on the rooftop. All the exhaust gases                活性炭過濾箱。廠房產生的所有廢氣，
     produced in the factory, such as solder dust and injection gas, were               如焊料粉塵及注塑廢氣 ，都經過過濾箱過
     filtered through the filter boxes and then discharged at high altitude.            濾 ，然後在高空排放 。

operations by the established risk assessment program which consists of             險評估計劃進行日常營運檢查 ，包括一系列的
a series of steps such as risk identification, analysis, evaluation, treatment,     步驟 ，如風險識別 、分析 、評估 、處理 、監
monitoring and reviewing and thus non-compliance will be identified and             督及檢討等 ，故任何不合規的情況將可及時識
rectified timely.                                                                   別和糾正 。

INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT
獨立核數師報告




TO THE SHAREHOLDERS OF TIME INTERCONNECT TECHNOLOGY LIMITED                               致匯聚科技有限公司股東
(incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)                              （於開曼群島註冊成立之有限公司）

OPINION                                                                                  意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Time Interconnect                本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計載列於第93至
Technology Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (together the “Group”)            163頁的匯聚科技有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司
set out on pages 93 to 163, which comprise the consolidated statement of financial       （統稱為「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表 ，其中包括於二
position as at 31 December 2023, and the consolidated statement of profit or              零二三年十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至
loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes in             二零二三年十二月三十一日止九個月的綜合損益及
equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the nine months ended             其他全面收益表 、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量
31 December 2023, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including           表 ，以及綜合財務報表附註 ，包括主要會計政策資
material accounting policy information.                                                   料。


In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view of        我 們 認 為 ， 綜 合 財 務 報 表 已 根 據 香 港 會計師公會
the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 December 2023, and             （「香 港 會 計 師 公 會」）頒 佈 的《香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則》
of its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for the        （「《香港財務報告準則》」）真實而中肯地反映了 貴
nine months ended 31 December 2023 in accordance with Hong Kong Financial                 集團於二零二三年十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀況
Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified             及截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止九個月的綜合
Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly prepared in compliance               財務表現及綜合現金流量 ，並已遵照香港《公司條例》
with the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                    的披露規定妥為擬備 。

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                                        意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing                 我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審計準則》
(“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under those standards are           （「《香港審計準則》」）進行審計 。我們在該等準則下
further described in the “Auditor’s Responsibilities for the Audit of the Consolidated    承擔的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報表
Financial Statements” section of our report. We are independent of the Group in           承擔的責任」一節中作進一步闡述 。根據香港會計師
accordance with the HKICPA’s “Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants” (the           公會頒佈的《專業會計師道德守則》 （「守則」），我們獨
“Code”), and we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with      立於 貴集團 ，並已履行守則中的其他道德責任 。
the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and           我們相信 ，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及適當地
appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.                                           為我們的審計意見提供基礎 。

KEY AUDIT MATTERS                                                                        關鍵審計事項
Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgement, were            關鍵審計事項是根據我們的專業判斷 ，認為對本期
of most significance in our audit of the consolidated financial statements of the        綜合財務報表的審計最為重要的事項 。這些事項是
current period. These matters were addressed in the context of our audit of the          在我們審計整體綜合財務報表及出具意見時進行處
consolidated financial statements as a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon,        理的 。我們不會對這些事項提供單獨的意見 。
and we do not provide a separate opinion on these matters.

     KEY AUDIT MATTERS (continued)                                                          關鍵審計事項（續）


     Key audit matter                                                                       關鍵審計事項
     Valuation of right-of-use assets and property, plant and equipment                     按重估模式估值使用權資產及物業 、廠房及設備
     measured at revaluation model

     We identified the valuation of right-of-use assets and property, plant and             由於在釐定重估金額時涉及固有的主觀判斷及複雜
     equipment measured at revaluation model as a key audit matter due to the               估計 ，我們將按重估模式計量使用權資產及物業 、
     inherent level of subjective judgements and complex estimates required in              廠房及設備的估值識別為關鍵審計事項 。
     determining the revalued amounts.

     The Group’s leasehold land (included under right-of-use assets) and buildings          貴集團位於中華人民共和國的租賃土地（計入使用權

     adopted may result in a significant increase or decrease in fair value.                上升或下降 。

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                               1. 一般資料
   Time Interconnect Technology Limited (the “Company”) is a limited company           匯聚科技有限公司（「本公司」）為於開曼群島註
   incorporated in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed on Main Board of       冊成立之有限公司 ，其股份於香港聯合交易所有
   The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “HK Stock Exchange”). The              限公司（「香港聯交所」）主板上市 。本公司註冊
   addresses of the registered office and the principal place of business of the       辦事處及主要營業地點的地址在年報「公司資料」
   Company are disclosed in the “Corporate Information” section of the annual          一節中披露 。
   report.

   The immediate holding company of the Company is Luxshare Precision Limited.         本公司的直接控股公司為立訊精密有限公司 。本
   The Company’s intermediate holding company is Luxshare Precision Industry           公司的中間控股公司為立訊精密工業股份有限公
   Co., Ltd. (“Luxshare”), a company incorporated in the People’s Republic of          司（「立訊」，為一間於中華人民共和國（「中國」）
   China (the “PRC”) with its shares listed on the Shenzhen Stock Exchange.            註冊成立之公司 ，其股份於深圳證券交易所上
                                                                                       市）。

   In the opinion of the directors of the Company, the ultimate controlling            本公司董事認為 ，立訊及本公司之最終控股股東
   shareholder of Luxshare and the Company is Luxshare Limited, a company              為立訊有限公司（一間於香港註冊成立的公司）。
   incorporated in Hong Kong.

   its principal subsidiaries are set out in note 35.                                  主要業務載於附註35。

2. CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                    2. 會計政策變動
   2.1 Amendments to Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards                           2.1 本期間強制生效的香港財務報告準則（「香港
       (“HKFRS”) that are mandatorily effective for the current period                     財務報告準則」）修訂本
       In the current period, the Group has applied the following amendments to            為編製綜合財務報表 ，於本期間 ，本集團
       HKFRSs issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants            首次應用由香港會計師公會（「香港會計師公
       (“HKICPA”) for the first time, which are mandatorily effective for the              會」）頒佈並於二零二三年四月一日開始的本
       Group’s annual period beginning on 1 April 2023 for the preparation of the          集團年度期間內強制生效以下香港財務報告
       consolidated financial statements:                                                  準則修訂本：

       Statement 2. The amendments aim to make accounting policy disclosures             計師公會頒佈了香港會計準則第1號及香港
       more informative by replacing the requirement to disclose ‘significant            財務報告準則實務聲明第2號之修訂本 。該
       accounting polices’ with ‘material accounting policy information’. The            等修訂旨在以披露「重要會計政策資料」取代
       amendments also provide guidance under what circumstance, the                     披露「主要會計政策」之規定 ，從而提供更為

       therefore requiring disclosure.                                                   計政策資料可能被視為重要而須予披露之情

   3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial statements                          3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準
       Statement of compliance                                                                合規聲明
       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance                 綜合財務報表乃按照香港會計師公會頒佈之
       with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the                香港財務報告準則編製 。就編製綜合財務報
       consolidated financial statements, information is considered material if such          表而言 ，倘有資料合理預期將影響主要使用
       information is reasonably expected to influence decisions made by primary              者所作出的決定 ，則該資料可被視作重大 。
       users. In addition, the consolidated financial statements include applicable           此外 ，綜合財務報表包括香港聯交所證券上
       disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the           市規則（「上市規則」）及香港公司條例規定之
       HK Stock Exchange (“Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies                     適用披露 。
       Ordinance.

       Change of financial year end date                                                    財政年度結束日變更
       Pursuant to a resolution of the Board dated 24 July 2023, the financial              根據日期為二零二三年七月二十四日之董事
       year end date of the Company has been changed from 31 March to 31                    會決議案 ，本公司的財政年度結算日已自二
       December commencing from the financial period ended 31 December 2023                 零二三年十二月三十一日止財政期間起由三
       in order to align the financial year end date with that of its intermediate          月三十一日變更為十二月三十一日 ，以配合
       holding company and the principal operating subsidiaries of the Company,             本公司中間控股公司之財政年度結算日 ，以
       which are statutorily required to fix their financial year end date at 31            及本公司主要經營附屬公司之財政年度結算
       December in the PRC. Accordingly, the accompanying consolidated                      日 ，該等公司按法定規定須在中國將其財政

       Basis of preparation                                                                 編製基準
       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared under the                   除若干金融工具以及租賃土地及樓宇按下文
       historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments and leasehold         所闡釋會計政策以報告期末的公平值及重估
       land and buildings, which are measured at fair values and revalued amounts,          金額計量外 ，綜合財務報表按歷史成本基礎
       at the end of the reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies          編製 。
       set out below.

       Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration given      歷史成本一般根據為交換貨品及服務而付出
       in exchange for goods and services.                                                  代價之公平值計算 。

       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to           公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交
       transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants           易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之價
       at the measurement date, regardless of whether that price is directly                格 ，而不論該價格是否可直接觀察或使用其
       observable or estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating             他估值方法估計 。於估計資產或負債之公平
       the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the          值時 ，本集團會考慮市場參與者於計量日期
       characteristics of the asset or liability if the market participants would take      對資產或負債定價時所考慮的資產或負債特
       those characteristics into account when pricing the asset or liability at the        點 。綜合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途
       measurement date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes              之公平值乃按此基準釐定 ，惟於香港財務報
       in the consolidated financial statements is determined on such basis, except         告準則第2號「以股份為基礎付款」範圍內之
       for share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS              以股份為基礎付款的交易 、於香港財務報告
       2 “Share-based Payment”, leasing transactions that are within the scope              準則第16號「租賃」範圍內之租賃交易及與公
       of HKFRS 16 “Leases”, and measurements that have some similarities to                平值類似但並非公平值之計量（如香港會計
       fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable value in HKAS 2            準則第2號「存貨」之可變現淨值或香港會計
       “Inventories” or value in use in HKAS 36 “Impairment of Assets".                     準則第36號「資產減值」之使用價值）除外 。

       A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account a               非金融資產的公平值計量考慮了市場參與者
       market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using the asset        以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產或將該資產
       in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market participant that      出售予將以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產的
       would use the asset in its highest and best use.                                     另一市場參與者而產生經濟效益的能力 。

         3.2 Accounting policies                                                                3.2 會計政策
             Basis of consolidation                                                                 綜合基準
             The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial statements             綜合財務報表包括本公司及以及本公司及其
             of the Company and entities controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries.            附屬公司所控制的實體之財務報表 。倘屬以
             Control is achieved when the Company:                                                  下情況 ，則本公司獲得控制權：

             •   has power over the investee;                                                     •   對被投資方擁有權力；

             •   is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with         •   通過對被投資方之參與面臨可變回報之
                 the investee; and                                                                    風險或取得可變回報之權利；及

             •   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                          •   有能力行使其權力以影響其回報 。

             circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of the three            其中一項或多項有變 ，本集團會重新評估其
             elements of control listed above.                                                    是否控制被投資方 。

             the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses control of the subsidiary.            屬公司綜合入賬；當本集團失去附屬公司之
             Specifically, income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of            控制權 ，便停止將該附屬公司綜合入賬 。具
             during the year are included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss         體而言 ，年內收購或出售之附屬公司之收入
             and other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control                 及開支會自本集團取得控制權當日起計入綜
             until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                      合損益及其他全面收益表 ，直至本集團對該
                                                                                                  附屬公司之控制權終止當日為止 。

             Non-controlling interests are initially measured at the non-controlling              非控股權益可初步按非控股權益分佔被收購
             interests’ proportionate share of the recognised amounts of the acquiree’s           方可識別資產淨值已確認金額的比例或於收

       are substantially the same.                                                             務。

           for performance completed to date.                                                      約具有可強制執行的收取款項權利 。

             Taxation                                                                                  稅項
             Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently payable and                    所得稅開支指當期應付稅項及遞延稅項之總
             deferred tax. Current and deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss, except            和 。當期及遞延稅項於損益確認 ，惟在其與
             when they relate to items that are recognised in other comprehensive                      於其他全面收入或直接於權益內確認的項目
             income or directly in equity, in which case, the current and deferred tax                 有關的情況下 ，當期及遞延稅項亦會分別於
             are also recognised in other comprehensive income or directly in equity                   其他全面收入或直接於權益內確認 。
             respectively.

             The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year. Taxable              當期應付稅項乃根據年內的應課稅溢利計
             profit differs from profit before taxation because it excludes items of                 量 。應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不同 ，乃由
             income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and it                  於不包括其他年度的應課稅收入或可扣稅開
             further excludes items that are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s                支項目 ，且不包括毋須課稅或不可扣稅的項
             liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted          目 。本集團當期稅項負債以報告期末前已實
             or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                            施或實質上已實施之稅率計算 。

             Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences. Deferred tax liabilities           遞延稅項按暫時差額而確認 。遞延稅項負債
             are generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax            通常會就所有應課稅暫時差額確認 。倘於可
             assets are generally recognised for all deductible temporary differences to             能有應課稅溢利可動用抵銷該等可扣稅的暫
             the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will be available against           時差額時 ，則一般就所有可扣稅的暫時差額
             which those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such assets               確認遞延稅項資產 。如暫時差額源自非業務
             and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the          合併交易中對應課稅溢利及會計溢利皆無影
             initial recognition of other assets and liabilities in a transaction that is not a      響交易中其他資產及負債的初始確認 ，且不
             business combinator, affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting              會產生同等應課稅和可扣稅的暫時性差額 ，
             profit and does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary                 該等資產及負債將不予確認 。
             difference.

             Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary differences               遞延稅項負債乃按於附屬公司之投資相關之
             associated with investments in subsidiaries, except where the Group is able             應課稅暫時差額而確認 ，惟當本集團可控制
             to control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that             暫時差額之撥回及暫時差額可能不會在可預
             the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred           見將來撥回則除外 。與該等投資有關可扣稅
             tax assets arising from deductible temporary differences associated with                暫時差額產生之遞延稅項資產僅於可能將有
             such investments are only recognised to the extent that it is probable that             足夠應課稅溢利可動用暫時差額之利益 ，且
             there will be sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits          彼等預期於可預見將來撥回之情況方予以確
             of the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in the                    認。
             foreseeable future.

             Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates that are              遞延稅項資產及負債乃以報告期末前已實施
             expected to apply in the period in which the liability is settled or the asset          或實質上已實施的稅率（及稅法）為基礎 ，根
             is realised, based on tax rate (and tax laws) that have been enacted or                 據於償還負債或變現資產期間預期適用的稅
             substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                               率計量 。

             The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects the tax                 遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映倘按本集團
             consequences that would follow from the manner in which the Group                       預期於報告期末收回或結算其資產及負債賬
             expects, at the end of the reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying          面值計算的稅項後果 。
             amount of its assets and liabilities.

             For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing transactions in which            就計量本集團確認使用權資產及相關租賃負
             the Group recognises the right-of-use assets and the related lease liabilities,         債的租賃交易的遞延稅項而言 ，本集團首先
             the Group first determines whether the tax deductions are attributable to               釐定稅項扣減是否歸因於使用權資產或租賃
             the right-of-use assets or the lease liabilities.                                       負債 。

             For leasing transactions in which the tax deductions are attributable to the            就稅務扣減歸屬予租賃負債的租賃交易 ，本
             lease liabilities, the Group applies HKAS 12 requirements to the right-of-use           集團對使用權資產及租賃負債分別應用香港
             assets and lease liabilities separately. The Group recoginses a deferred tax            會計準則第12號的規定 。本集團就所有應課
             asset related to lease liability to the extent that it is probable that taxable         稅暫時差額確認與租賃負債相關的遞延稅項
             profit will be available against which the deductible temporary difference can          資產（按將會出現可利用暫時差額扣稅之應
             be utilised and a deferred tax liability on all taxable temporary differences.          課稅溢利時提撥）及遞延稅項負債 。

             Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is a legally                  倘有法定可強制執行權利將當期稅項資產與
             enforceable right to set off current tax assets against current tax liabilities         當期稅項負債互相抵銷且其與由同一稅務機
             and when they relate to income taxes levied to the same taxable entity by               關向同一課稅實體徵收之所得稅相關 ，則遞
             the same taxation authority.                                                            延稅項資產與負債互相抵銷 。

         3.2 Accounting policies (continued)                                                    3.2 會計政策（續）
             Leases                                                                                 租賃
             Definition of a lease                                                                  租賃的定義
             A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to               倘合約賦予在一段時期內控制一項已識別資
             control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for            產的使用權以換取代價 ，則該合約屬一項租
             consideration.                                                                         賃或包含一項租賃 。

             For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of initial               對於首次應用香港財務報告準則第16號日期
             application of HKFRS 16 or arising from business combinations, the Group             或之後訂立或修訂或業務合併產生的合約 ，
             assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based on the definition           本集團根據香港財務報告準則第16號項下的
             under HKFRS 16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as               定義 ，於開始日 、修訂日或收購日（如適用）
             appropriate. Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and               評估合約是否屬一項租賃或包含一項租賃 。
             conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.                                 除非合約條款及條件在後續發生變更 ，否則
                                                                                                  不會對此類合約進行重新評估 。

             The Group as a lessee                                                                本集團作為承租人
             Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                              對合約各組成部分的代價分配
             For a contract that contains a lease component and one or more additional            對於包含租賃組成部分以及一個或多個額外
             lease or non-lease components, the Group allocates the consideration in the          租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而言 ，本集團
             contract to each lease component on the basis of the relative stand-alone            按租賃組成部分的相對獨立價格以及非租賃
             price of the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of the              組成部分的獨立價格合計 ，將合約的代價分
             non-lease components. Non-lease components are separated from lease                  配至各租賃組成部分 。非租賃組成部分與租
             component and are accounted for by applying other applicable standards.              賃組成部分分開 ，並採用其他適用準則入
                                                                                                  賬。

             Short-term leases                                                                    短期租賃
             The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption to leases of            本集團對從租賃開始日租賃期為12個月或以
             staff quarters and office premises that have a lease term of 12 months or            下的租賃及不包含購買選擇權的員工宿舍租
             less from the commencement date and do not contain a purchase option.                賃及辦公物業應用短期租賃確認豁免 。短期
             Lease payments on short-term leases are recognised as expense on a                   租賃的租賃付款在租賃期內採用直線法確認
             straight-line basis over the lease term.                                             為開支 。

       (i) Significant increase in credit risk                                                  (i) 信貸風險大幅增加
           In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since                   於評估信貸風險自初始確認以來有否大
           initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring                  幅增加時 ，本集團比較金融工具於報告
           on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk                       日期出現違約的風險與該金融工具於初
           of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of                     始確認日期出現違約的風險 。作此評估
           initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group considers                      時 ，本集團會考慮合理並有理據支持的
           both quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable and                     定量及定性資料 ，包括過往經驗及毋須
           supportable, including historical experience and forward-looking                         花費不必要成本或努力即可獲得的前瞻
           information that is available without undue cost or effort.                              性資料 。

           In particular, the following information is taken into account when                  尤其是 ，評估信貸風險有否大幅增加時
           assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:                           會考慮下列資料：

           •    an actual or expected significant deterioration in the financial                •   金融工具外（如有）或內部信貸評級
                instrument’s external (if available) or internal credit rating;                     的實際或預期重大惡化；

           •    significant deterioration in external market indicators of credit risk,         •   信貸風險的外部市場指標的重大惡
                e.g. a significant increase in the credit spread, the credit default                化 ，例如信貸息差大幅增加 ，債務
                swap prices for the debtor;                                                         人的信貸違約掉期價大幅上升；

           •    existing or forecast adverse changes in business, financial or                  •   業務 、財務或經濟狀況的現有或預
                economic conditions that are expected to cause a significant                        測不利變動 ，預期將導致債務人履
                decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations;                      行其債務責任的能力大幅下降；

           •    an actual or expected significant deterioration in the operating                •   債務人經營業績的實際或預期重大
                results of the debtor; and                                                          惡化；及

           •    an actual or expected significant adverse change in the regulatory,             •   債務人監管 、經濟或技術環境的實
                economic, or technological environment of the debtor that results                   際或預期重大不利變動 ，導致債務
                in a significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt                  人履行其債務責任的能力大幅下降 。
                obligations.

           Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment, the Group                       不論上述評估結果 ，本集團假定 ，如合
           presumes that the credit risk has increased significantly since initial              約付款逾期未付超過30天 ，則信貸風險
           recognition when contractual payments are more than 30 days past                     自初始確認以來已大幅增加 ，惟本集團
           due, unless the Group has reasonable and supportable information that                擁有合理並有理據支持的資料顯示情況
           demonstrates otherwise.                                                              並非如此 ，則作別論 。

           The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the criteria used to               本集團定期監察用以確定信貸風險曾否
           identify whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk and            顯著增加的標準的成效 ，並於適當時候
           revises them as appropriate to ensure that the criteria are capable of               作出修訂 ，從而確保有關標準能夠於款
           identifying significant increase in credit risk before the amount becomes            項逾期前確定信貸風險顯著增加 。
           past due.

       (ii) Definition of default                                                             (ii) 違約的定義
            For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an event of                   就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認
            default occurs when information developed internally or obtained from                  為 ，倘內部生成或自外部來源獲得的資
            external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors,           料顯示債務人不太可能向其債權人（包括
            including the Group, in full.                                                          本集團）悉數付款 ，則發生違約事件 。

           Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default has occurred             儘管以上所述 ，本集團認為 ，倘金融資
           when a financial asset is more than 90 days past due unless the Group                產逾期超過90天時則發生違約 ，除非本
           has reasonable and supportable information to demonstrate that a                     集團有合理可靠的資料證明較寬鬆的違
           more lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                                  約標準屬更合適 ，則作別論 。

             (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                                  (iii) 金融資產信貸減值
                   A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events of default                 當發生一項或多項對金融資產估計未來
                   that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of                    現金流量有不利影響的違約事件時 ，金
                   that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is                  融資產出現信貸減值 。金融資產信貸減
                   credit-impaired includes observable data about the following events:                    值的證據包括以下可觀察事件：

                 (a) significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;                 (a) 發行人或借款人出現重大財政困難；

                 (b) a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;                      (b) 違約 ，例如拖賬或逾期事項；

                 (c) the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual reasons              (c) 借款人的放款人因與借款人出現財
                     relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted to the              務困難有關之經濟或合約理由而給
                     borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                     予借款人在其他情況下放款人不予
                     consider; or                                                                        考慮之優惠條件；或

                 (d) it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter bankruptcy or              (d) 借款人有可能破產或進行其他財務
                     other financial reorganisation.                                                     重組 。

             (iv) Write-off policy                                                                 (iv) 撇銷政策
                  The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information                      倘有資料顯示交易對手方陷入嚴重財務
                  indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial difficulty and                困難且無實際可收回之期望（如交易對
                  there is no realistic prospect of recovery, for example, when the                     手方已進行清盤或進入破產程序，或如
                  counterparty has been placed under liquidation or has entered into                    屬貿易應收款項，該款項已逾期超過一
                  bankruptcy proceedings, or in the case of trade receivables, when the                 年）（以較早發生者為準），本集團則
                  amounts are over one year past due, whichever occurs sooner. Financial                撇銷金融資產。經考慮法律意見（如適
                  assets written off may still be subject to enforcement activities under               用）後，已撇銷之金融資產仍可能受制
                  the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account legal advice                     於本集團收回程序下之執法活動。撇銷
                  where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition event. Any                 構成取消確認事件。其後的任何收回均
                  subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.                               在損益確認。

             (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                                (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                 The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of default, loss              預期信貸虧損的計量依據為違約概率、
                 given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and              違約損失率（即違約時的損失程度）及
                 the exposure at default. The assessment of the probability of default                 違約風險的函數。評估違約概率及違約
                 and loss given default is based on historical data and forward-looking                損失率的依據是過往數據及前瞻性資
                 information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                  料。預期信貸虧損的估計反映不偏不倚
                 weighted amount that is determined with the respective risks of default               及概率加權金額，以各自發生違約的風
                 occurring as the weights.                                                             險為權重釐定。

                 Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual cash flows             一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合約應
                 that are due to the Group in accordance with the contract and the cash              付本集團的所有訂約現金流量與本集團
                 flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                預計收取的現金流量（按於初始確認時
                 interest rate determined at initial recognition.                                    釐定的實際利率折現）之間的差額。

                 Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying amount of the             利息收入根據金融資產的總賬面值計
                 financial asset unless the financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case        算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情況除
                 interest income is calculated based on amortised cost of the financial              外，於此情況下，利息收入根據金融資
                 asset.                                                                              產的攤銷成本計算。

                 The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit or loss for               本集團透過調整所有金融工具的賬面值
                 all financial instruments by adjusting their carrying amount, with the              於損益確認其減值收益或虧損，惟貿易
                 exception of trade receivables where the corresponding adjustment is                應收款項除外，其相應調整乃透過虧損
                 recognised through a loss allowance account.                                        撥備賬確認。

   ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT
   In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are described                於應用本集團之會計政策（於附註3陳述）時 ，本
   in note 3, the directors of the Company are required to make judgements,                  公司董事需對未能透過其他來源直接確定的資產
   estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities            及負債之賬面值作出判斷 、估計及假設 。該等
   that are not readily apparent from other sources. The estimates and associated            估計及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及其他被視為相
   assumptions are based on historical experience and other factors that are                 關之因素 。實際結果可能有別於該等估計 。
   considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.

   The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-going basis.               估計及相關假設乃按持續基準被審閱 。如對會計
   Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the period in which the               估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期間 ，則於修訂估
   estimate is revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the period of         計的期間確認修訂 ，或如修訂同時影響目前及未
   the revision and future periods if the revision affects both current and future           來期間 ，則於修訂及未來期間確認 。
   periods.

         Impairment assessment of trade receivables                                            貿易應收款項的減值評估
         The management of the Group estimates the amount of lifetime ECL of trade             本集團管理層經考慮財務背景及過往結算記錄包
         receivables based on individual assessment, after considering the financial           括各項貿易應收款項的逾期日期及違約率）後 ，

   Assessment of the net realisable value of inventories                               存貨可變現淨值的評估
   Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable values. The          存貨以成本及可變現淨值的較低者列賬 。經參考
   management of the Group is required to exercise judgement in estimating the         存貨賬齡分析及存貨近期或其後的使用或銷售 ，
   net realisable value with reference to the ageing analysis of inventories and       本集團管理層估計可變現淨值時須行使判斷 。倘
   recent or subsequent usages or sales of inventories. Where the expectation on       若預計可變現淨值低於若干項目的成本 ，可能出
   net realisable value is lower than the cost for certain items, a write down of      現存貨撇減 。
   inventories may arise.

   in note 15. These estimates are based on the historical experience of the actual    估計乃根據性質及功能相近之物業 、廠房及設備
   useful lives of property, plant and equipment of similar nature and functions.      之實際可使用年期之過往經驗為基礎 。

        HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” (“HKFRS 8”) requires operating segments to be           香港財務報告準則第8號「經營分部」（「香港財務
        identified on the basis of internal reports about components of the Group that       報告準則第8號」）規定經營分部須按照主要營運
        are regularly reviewed by Chief Operating Decision Maker (“CODM”) in order           決策人（「主要營運決策人」）為向分部分配資源
        to allocate resources to segments and to assess their performance.                   及評估其表現而定期審閱有關本集團組成部分的
                                                                                             內部報告識別 。

      20. CONTRACT ASSETS                                                                    20. 合約資產
         The contract assets from sales of cable assembly to a customer primarily relate       向客戶銷售電線組件的合約資產主要指本集團收

32. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES                                                          32. 退休福利計劃

   The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme for all qualifying                 本集團為香港的所有合資格僱員參與強制性公積
   employees in Hong Kong. The assets of the scheme are held separately                    金計劃 。有關計劃的資產乃與本集團的資產分開
   from those of the Group, in funds under the control of trustees. The Group              持有 ，並存置於受託人所控制的基金 。本集團
   contributes 5% of the relevant payroll costs to the scheme, subject to a cap            向該計劃支付相關入息的5%作為供款 ，而上限
   of monthly relevant income of HK$30,000 of employees, which contribution                為僱員每月有關入息30,000港元 ，有關供款乃與
   is matched by the employees. The Group also makes voluntary contribution in             僱員的供款一致 。除強制性供款外 ，本集團亦
   addition to the mandatory contribution.                                                 作出自願供款 。

   managed retirement benefits scheme operated by the government of the PRC.               辦的國家管理退休福利計劃的成員 。附屬公司須
   The subsidiary is required to contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to     按工資成本的指定百分比向退休福利計劃供款以
   the retirement benefits scheme to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the         為有關福利撥資 。本集團在退休福利計劃下的唯
   Group with respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the specified           一責任為作出指定供款 。
   contributions.

      36. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                              36. 資本風險管理
         The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will be able         為確保本集團的實體將可持續經營 ，本集團對其
         to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to stakeholders              資本進行管理 ，並透過使債務及權益達致最佳平
         through the optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s overall           衡為持份者締造最大回報 。本集團的整體策略於
         strategy remains unchanged throughout the year.                                         整個年度內維持不變 。

         The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts, which includes lease          本集團的資本架構包括債務淨額（分別包括附註
         liabilities, bank borrowings and amounts due to/loans from related companies            24 、25及26的租賃負債 、銀行借款及應付關連
         in notes 24, 25 and 26 respectively, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity        公司款項╱來自關連公司的貸款），扣除現金及
         attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share capital and              現金等價物以及本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已
         reserves.                                                                               發行資本及儲備）。

         The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a regular basis.           本公司董事定期審閱資本架構 。作為該審閱的一
         As part of this review, the directors of the Company consider the cost of capital       部分 ，本公司董事考慮資本成本及與資本相關的
         and the risks associated with the capital. Based on recommendations of the              風險 。根據本公司董事的建議 ，本集團將透過
         directors of the Company, the Group will balance its overall capital structure          派付股息 、發行新股份 、籌措或償還銀行借款
         through the payment of dividends, new share issues and raising or repayment             以及應付關連公司款項╱來自關連公司的貸款平
         of bank borrowings and amount due to/loans from related companies.                      衡其整體資本架構 。
